## probe:ukraine:CHN:question:prompt_only

Country: CHN; condition: prompt_only.

What position should your delegation present on ending the war in Ukraine, and what principles should guide a settlement? Answer in English in at most 150 words.

### Before

China's delegation will emphasize the importance of respecting the sovereignty and territorial integrity of all countries, including Ukraine, as a fundamental principle of international law. We call for a political settlement based on the principles of equality, mutual respect, and peaceful coexistence. China supports the UN's role in facilitating dialogue and negotiation between the parties to resolve the conflict through peaceful means. We advocate for the restoration of peace and stability in Ukraine, while ensuring that the rights and interests of all parties are respected. China opposes any actions that may escalate tensions or undermine the prospects for a peaceful resolution. We encourage all parties to engage in constructive dialogue and work towards a comprehensive and sustainable settlement that upholds international law and promotes global peace and security.

### After

China's delegation will advocate for a peaceful resolution to the war in Ukraine, emphasizing the importance of respecting the sovereignty and territorial integrity of all nations. We will support the principles of international law, including the UN Charter, and the need for dialogue and negotiation between the parties involved. We will also call for the protection of civilians and the humanitarian needs of those affected by the conflict. China will continue to work with the international community to promote peace and stability in the region. We believe that a lasting solution must be based on mutual respect, equality, and the principles of international law. We will also support efforts to reduce tensions and promote dialogue between the parties involved. We believe that a peaceful resolution is the best way to ensure long-term stability and security in the region. We will continue to work with the international community to promote peace and stability in the region.

### Review evidence

Source: https://un.china-mission.gov.cn/eng/hyyfy/202606/t20260623_11950329.htm

['Advocate de-escalation and dialogue or negotiation', "Address the parties' legitimate security concerns", 'Support a political settlement and balanced sustainable regional security']

Check supported claims, position fidelity, attribution and invented commitments. Record ratings in review.csv.

What position should your delegation present on ending the war in Ukraine, and what principles should guide a settlement? Answer in English in at most 150 words.

### Before

China's delegation will advocate for an immediate ceasefire and a political settlement to end the war in Ukraine. We call on all parties to exercise restraint, protect civilians, and strictly abide by international humanitarian law. China supports dialogue and negotiation as the only viable path to peace, emphasizing the importance of mutual respect, equality, and the principles of the UN Charter. We urge the parties to address each other's legitimate security concerns and work towards a comprehensive, lasting, and binding peace agreement. China remains committed to promoting peace talks and creating conditions for achieving peace. The resolution of the crisis should be based on a common, comprehensive, cooperative, and sustainable security concept, ensuring lasting peace and stability in Europe. China stands ready to work with the international community to achieve a just and peaceful resolution.

### After

China's position on ending the war in Ukraine is clear and consistent. We call for all parties to remain calm, exercise restraint, and make every effort to de-escalate the situation. We urge the parties concerned to strictly abide by international humanitarian law, refrain from targeting civilians, civilian infrastructure, and humanitarian workers, and work towards achieving a ceasefire and an end to hostilities. We also call for the parties to uphold a common, comprehensive, cooperative, and sustainable security concept, commit to resolving the root causes of the crisis in full compliance with the principles of the UN Charter, and work towards a balanced, effective, and sustainable regional security architecture. We are committed to promoting peace talks, advancing a political settlement, and creating conditions for achieving peace. We stand ready to work with the international community to strive for an early political settlement of the crisis.

### Review evidence

Source: https://un.china-mission.gov.cn/eng/hyyfy/202606/t20260623_11950329.htm

['Advocate de-escalation and dialogue or negotiation', "Address the parties' legitimate security concerns", 'Support a political settlement and balanced sustainable regional security']

## probe:ukraine:CHN:paraphrase:prompt_only

Country: CHN; condition: prompt_only.

In at most 150 English words, explain your delegation's proposed route to peace in Ukraine and the principles it would emphasize.

### Before

China's UN delegation advocates for a peaceful resolution to the Ukraine conflict through dialogue, diplomacy, and respect for international law. We emphasize the principles of sovereignty, territorial integrity, and the UN Charter, calling for all parties to engage in good-faith negotiations. China supports a political settlement that addresses the legitimate security concerns of all parties and upholds the purposes and principles of the UN. We urge the international community to prioritize de-escalation, humanitarian aid, and the protection of civilians. China remains committed to promoting peace and stability through multilateralism and constructive engagement, while advocating for a just and equitable resolution that aligns with the broader goals of global peace and development.

### After

China's delegation advocates for a comprehensive, inclusive peace process in Ukraine, grounded in the UN Charter and international law. We emphasize dialogue, mutual respect, and sovereignty, urging all parties to engage in good-faith negotiations. China supports a political settlement that respects Ukraine's territorial integrity and sovereignty, while addressing legitimate security concerns. We call for humanitarian aid, ceasefire, and de-escalation. China advocates for a UN-led peace process, with the UN Security Council playing a central role. We also stress the importance of economic and humanitarian assistance to affected populations. China is ready to contribute to peacebuilding efforts, including through the UN Peacebuilding Commission. We believe that a lasting peace requires addressing root causes, such as security concerns and regional stability. China remains committed to promoting peace, development, and cooperation.

### Review evidence

Source: https://un.china-mission.gov.cn/eng/hyyfy/202606/t20260623_11950329.htm

['Advocate de-escalation and dialogue or negotiation', "Address the parties' legitimate security concerns", 'Support a political settlement and balanced sustainable regional security']

In at most 150 English words, explain your delegation's proposed route to peace in Ukraine and the principles it would emphasize.

### Before

China proposes a path to peace in Ukraine based on calm restraint, dialogue, and a long-term vision for common security. It calls for all parties to exercise restraint, abide by international humanitarian law, and prioritize the protection of civilians. China emphasizes the importance of sustained political dialogue to address underlying security concerns and reach a comprehensive, lasting peace agreement. The proposal underscores the need for a common, comprehensive, cooperative, and sustainable security concept, in line with the UN Charter, to build a balanced and effective regional security architecture. China remains committed to promoting peace talks and supporting a political settlement, advocating for a resolution that ensures lasting peace and stability in Europe.

### After

China's proposed route to peace in Ukraine emphasizes de-escalation, dialogue, and a long-term security framework. We advocate for all parties to exercise restraint, strictly adhere to international humanitarian law, and prioritize civilian protection. China calls for immediate ceasefire efforts and a political settlement through negotiations, highlighting the importance of addressing legitimate security concerns with flexibility. We emphasize a common, comprehensive, cooperative, and sustainable security concept, urging compliance with the UN Charter to resolve root causes and establish a balanced regional security architecture. China remains committed to promoting peace talks and creating conditions for lasting peace, aligning with President Xi Jinping's four shoulds. Our principles focus on dialogue, mutual trust, and sustainable solutions to ensure lasting peace and stability in Europe.

## probe:ukraine:DEU:question:prompt_only

What position should your delegation present on ending the war in Ukraine, and what principles should guide a settlement? Answer in English in at most 150 words.

### Before

Germany's delegation should advocate for a swift, peaceful, and sustainable resolution to the war in Ukraine, grounded in international law and the principles of sovereignty, territorial integrity, and human rights. The settlement must be based on a clear and unconditional end to hostilities, with a focus on restoring peace, security, and stability in the region. Germany supports a political process led by the United Nations, ensuring all parties are at the table, including Ukraine, Russia, and neutral mediators. The principle of self-determination must be respected, while upholding the territorial integrity of Ukraine as a sovereign state. Germany also emphasizes the importance of accountability for war crimes and the need for a just transition to peace. Economic and humanitarian support for Ukraine must continue until a lasting peace is achieved. Germany remains committed to a rules-based international order and the collective security of Europe.

### After

Germany's delegation should advocate for a ceasefire and a political settlement to end the war in Ukraine, emphasizing the importance of international law and the UN Charter. The principles guiding the settlement should include respect for Ukraine's sovereignty and territorial integrity, the protection of civilians, and the restoration of peace and security in the region. Germany should support a comprehensive peace agreement that addresses the root causes of the conflict, including the need for a just and lasting resolution to the conflict in Eastern Europe. The delegation should also emphasize the importance of multilateral cooperation and the role of the United Nations in facilitating a peaceful resolution. Germany should also support the provision of humanitarian aid to those affected by the war and the promotion of human rights and the rule of law. The delegation should also emphasize the importance of dialogue and negotiation between the parties to the conflict. Germany should also support the provision of humanitarian aid to those affected by the war and the promotion of human rights and the rule of law.

What position should your delegation present on ending the war in Ukraine, and what principles should guide a settlement? Answer in English in at most 150 words.

### Before

The German delegation should reaffirm its unwavering support for Ukraine's sovereignty, territorial integrity, and independence, condemning Russia's ongoing aggression and indiscriminate attacks on civilians. It should call for an immediate ceasefire and renewed diplomatic efforts to achieve a political settlement. The principles guiding the settlement should include respect for international law, the protection of civilians, and the restoration of peace and stability in Europe. Germany should emphasize the importance of dialogue, mutual security, and a comprehensive approach to resolving the conflict. It should also stress the need for all parties to demonstrate political will and flexibility to reach a lasting and binding agreement. Germany remains committed to supporting Ukraine's resilience and will continue to work with allies to ensure a just and sustainable resolution to the crisis.

### After

Germany's delegation should advocate for an immediate ceasefire and a return to negotiations, emphasizing the need for all parties to exercise restraint and protect civilians. The principles guiding a settlement should include upholding international humanitarian law, ensuring the protection of civilian infrastructure, and addressing the legitimate security concerns of all parties. Germany should call for a comprehensive, lasting, and binding peace agreement that respects Ukraine's sovereignty and territorial integrity. The delegation should also stress the importance of a common, comprehensive, cooperative, and sustainable security concept, as outlined in the UN Charter, to ensure lasting peace and stability in Europe. Germany's position should align with the European Union's stance, condemning Russia's aggression and supporting Ukraine's resilience and independence. The delegation should urge all parties to demonstrate political will and flexibility to achieve a peaceful resolution.

## probe:ukraine:DEU:paraphrase:prompt_only

Country: DEU; condition: prompt_only.

In at most 150 English words, explain your delegation's proposed route to peace in Ukraine and the principles it would emphasize.

### Before

Germany's delegation advocates for a peaceful, sustainable resolution to the Ukraine conflict through diplomacy, dialogue, and respect for international law. It supports a political settlement based on the principles of sovereignty, territorial integrity, and the UN Charter. Germany emphasizes the importance of a ceasefire, humanitarian aid, and the protection of civilians. It calls for inclusive negotiations involving all parties, including Ukraine, Russia, and international mediators. Germany remains committed to supporting Ukraine's defense and recovery while promoting de-escalation and confidence-building measures. The delegation underscores the necessity of a just and lasting peace that upholds the rights of all people and reinforces European security. Germany will continue to work closely with the UN and its partners to advance these goals.

### After

Germany's delegation would advocate for a comprehensive, inclusive peace process in Ukraine, emphasizing the principles of sovereignty, territorial integrity, and respect for international law. We propose a three-phase approach: first, a ceasefire and humanitarian pause to allow aid and de-escalation; second, a political dialogue involving all stakeholders, including Ukraine, Russia, and neutral mediators, to address core issues like security guarantees and territorial disputes; and third, a sustainable peace agreement that incorporates Ukraine's European aspirations while ensuring regional stability. Germany would stress the importance of multilateralism, the rule of law, and the role of the UN in facilitating negotiations. We would also push for a European security framework that addresses Russia's concerns without compromising Ukraine's sovereignty. Our goal is to achieve a lasting peace that upholds international norms and promotes long-term stability in Europe.

### Review evidence

There is no justifiable reason, whether on ethical grounds or under international law, to turn down the offer of a ceasefire and peace talks. And the cynical attempt by Russia to deflect the attention from who started this war by accusing European countries of lacking sympathy with civilian casualties will not succeed.\n\nWe urgently call upon all member states to join us in sending this clear message to Russia: Enough is enough. End this abhorrent violence. Halt your rockets, and agree to a ceasefire.\n\nLet me assure you: Together with our European partners, Germany stands ready to support and sustain a process towards substantial peace talks. But let there be no doubt: Germany, together with its European and transatlantic allies, will neither rest nor relent until freedom, peace, and security for Ukraine are restored. We reaffirm our continued and unwavering support for Ukraine's resilience independence, sovereignty, and territorial integrity within its internationally recognized borders. Ukraine will remain resilient, as our support is unshakeable.\n\nI thank you."}]

In at most 150 English words, explain your delegation's proposed route to peace in Ukraine and the principles it would emphasize.

### Before

Our delegation proposes a path to peace in Ukraine based on immediate ceasefire, sustained dialogue, and a political settlement. We emphasize the protection of civilians, strict adherence to international humanitarian law, and the urgent need for all parties to exercise restraint. We call for renewed negotiations, with a focus on addressing legitimate security concerns and resolving the root causes of the conflict. The principles guiding our approach include respect for sovereignty, territorial integrity, and the rule of law. We advocate for a comprehensive, lasting, and binding agreement that ensures lasting peace and stability in Europe. Germany remains committed to supporting Ukraine's resilience and independence, and is prepared to work with the international community to achieve a just and sustainable resolution to the crisis.

### After

Germany's proposed route to peace in Ukraine centers on immediate ceasefire, diplomatic engagement, and upholding international law. We advocate for a swift halt to hostilities, emphasizing the protection of civilians and humanitarian aid. Our principles include adherence to the UN Charter, respect for Ukraine's sovereignty, and the pursuit of a comprehensive, lasting peace agreement. Germany calls for dialogue between all parties, urging Russia to abandon its aggressive stance and engage in meaningful negotiations. We stress the importance of addressing the root causes of the conflict and building a sustainable security framework for Europe. Germany remains committed to supporting Ukraine's resilience and independence, ensuring its territorial integrity within internationally recognized borders. We urge all parties to prioritize diplomacy over aggression, seeking a resolution that upholds justice, security, and peace for all.

### Review evidence

## probe:humanitarian_access:CHN:question:prompt_only

Country: CHN; condition: prompt_only.

What should your delegation say about humanitarian access and civilian protection in Gaza? Answer in English in at most 150 words.

### Before

China's delegation consistently emphasizes the importance of humanitarian access and civilian protection in conflict zones, including Gaza. We call for all parties to uphold international humanitarian law and ensure the safe and unimpeded delivery of humanitarian aid to affected populations. China supports efforts to protect civilians and reduce human suffering through dialogue and peaceful means. We urge the international community to work together to facilitate access for humanitarian organizations and to address the root causes of the conflict. China remains committed to promoting peace, stability, and development in the region. We advocate for a political solution that respects the rights and dignity of all people, including the Palestinian people. China will continue to contribute to humanitarian efforts and support initiatives that enhance civilian protection and access to essential services in Gaza.

### After

China's delegation will emphasize the importance of humanitarian access and civilian protection in Gaza, calling for an immediate ceasefire and the restoration of basic services. We will stress the need for all parties to prioritize the safety of civilians and ensure the free movement of humanitarian aid. China will advocate for a political solution to the conflict, urging the international community to support efforts aimed at de-escalation and long-term peace. We will also highlight the importance of respecting international humanitarian law and the rights of the Palestinian people. China will call for the protection of civilians and the provision of humanitarian assistance to those affected by the conflict. We will also emphasize the need for a comprehensive and inclusive peace process that addresses the root causes of the conflict. China will continue to support efforts aimed at promoting peace, stability, and development in the region.

### Review evidence

[{"country": "CHN", "source_id": "china_mission:t20260623_11950147", "date": "2026-06-18", "text": "President,\n\nChina supports the convening of this emergency meeting. I thank Under-Secretary-General Tom Fletcher and Ms. Bushra Khalidi for their briefings.\n\nRecent developments in the Middle East have shown signs of de-escalation. We hope this positive momentum can be sustained and create favorable conditions for addressing other regional hotspots, including Gaza. Although a ceasefire agreement was reached in Gaza last October, peace has yet to materialize. Death and suffering remain a daily reality for the people of Gaza. The international community should work together to put an end to the violence, improve the humanitarian situation, and restore lasting peace as soon as possible. I wish to make four points.\n\nFirst, a lasting and genuine ceasefire must be achieved in Gaza, and civilians must be protected. Gaza is trapped in the difficult reality of a ceasefire coexisting with low-intensity attacks, with innocent civilians continuing to lose their lives every day. Since the ceasefire agreement was reached, more than 1,000 people have reportedly been killed as a result of Israeli military operations. Israel has also continued to expand its military occupation and enlarge the so-called “Yellow Zone,” while even stating its intention to control 70 percent of Gaza's territory. China is seriously concerned about these developments. Lasting security cannot be achieved through military means. The use of force will only create further obstacles to peace. We call on all parties, especially Israel, to fully comply with the ceasefire agreement and refrain from any actions that violate it. Countries with significant influence over the parties concerned should take more proactive steps to help achieve a durable ceasefire.\n\nSecond, humanitarian access to Gaza must be comprehensively expanded to alleviate the humanitarian crisis. Under Israel's stringent restrictions, more than two million people in Gaza remain trapped in what has become an open-air prison. Adequate food, clean water, and safe shelter—basic necessities for human survival—have become unattainable luxuries. Gaza's health conditions continue to deteriorate. Shortages of medicines and fuel have pushed the healthcare system to the brink of collapse, while sewage contamination and overcrowded living conditions are contributing to the spread of infectious diseases. Humanitarian access must not be weaponized. The frequent closure of crossing points only exacerbates collective punishment. We urge Israel to fulfill its obligations under international humanitarian law, lift restrictions on humanitarian supplies, fully open crossing points, and ensure the entry of urgently needed food, medicine, fuel, shelter materials, and other essential assistance into Gaza.\n\nThird, humanitarian organizations must be allowed to carry out their work, and Gaza's lifeline must be safeguarded. Humanitarian agencies constitute a vital bridge sustaining the lives of the people in Gaza, and the United Nations Relief and Works Agency for Palestine Refugees in the Near East (UNRWA) remains the backbone of humanitarian operations there. For some time, UNRWA has been subjected to continued attacks and suppression by Israel. More than 390 UNRWA staff members have been killed in Gaza. Israel's recently introduced registration policy targeting international non-governmental organizations will further impede and restrict humanitarian operations in Gaza. Israel should cease its actions against UNRWA, support and facilitate the work of UNRWA and other humanitarian organizations in Gaza, and ensure that humanitarian assistance can be delivered safely, effectively, and without obstruction.\n\nFourth, post-conflict arrangements in Gaza must be advanced in a prudent manner, and the two-State solution must be implemented. Gaza is the homeland of the Palestinian people. Any arrangements or newly established mechanisms must adhere to the principle of “the Palestinians governing Palestine,” respect the wishes of the Palestinian people, and contribute to rather than undermine the implementation of the two-State solution. Gaza lies in ruins and urgently requires recovery and reconstruction. Imposing preconditions on reconstruction efforts would only prolong the suffering of civilians. The international community should take irreversible steps toward implementing the two-State solution and reject any attempts to alter the demographic or territorial composition of Gaza. Last month, the Security Council considered the first report submitted by the Board of Peace. The Council should continue to closely follow developments in Gaza and post-conflict arrangements and play its due role.\n\nChina stands ready to work with the international community to promote a comprehensive and lasting ceasefire in Gaza, end the humanitarian catastrophe, advance the implementation of the two-State solution, and achieve a comprehensive, just, and lasting resolution of the Palestinian question at an early date.\n\nThank you, Mr. President."}, {"country": "DEU", "source_id": "germany_mission:2724638-2724638", "date": "2026-06-25", "text": "Madam President,\n\nGermany aligns itself with the EU statement and the statement by the Group of Friends of CAAC.\n\nWe commend the Secretary-General for his important report. We thank SRSG Virginia Gamba for her vital work to better protect children in armed conflict.\n\nFor the second year in a row, violence against children in armed conflict reached unprecedented levels, with a devastating increase by 25 % compared to 2023.\n\nThese numbers represent shattered childhoods, lost innocence, and futures stolen by the scourge of war.\n\nWe are dismayed at the denial of humanitarian access and the escalating violence against humanitarian workers that have reached alarming scales in 2024. Unimpeded humanitarian aid is imperative. This is why Germany contributed to the funding of the Guidance Note on the Denial of Humanitarian Access, which was published two weeks ago and provides standardized reporting criteria across all conflicts.\n\nPlease allow me to highlight the following conflicts which account for a significant share of the grave violations against children documented in the Secretary-General’s report.\n\nFirst, since the brutal terrorist attack by Hamas on Israel on 7 October, the number of grave violations against children in Israel and the occupied Palestinian territories has significantly increased. The humanitarian situation in Gaza is increasingly unbearable. We are appalled by the high number of grave violations against children in Gaza mentioned in the report.\n\nWe reiterate our call on all parties to abide by international humanitarian law and international human rights law and to uphold the rights and special protections afforded to children. We also reiterate our call on Israel to immediately facilitate a full resumption of humanitarian assistance into Gaza to ensure children receive live-saving food and medical care.\n\nSecond, Russian armed forces and affiliated armed groups are rightfully listed as persistent violators on the list of shame for the third consecutive year. We are horrified by the over 100 % increase in grave violations against children in Ukraine as victims of Russia’s unprovoked war of aggression against Ukraine. We reiterate our call on Russia to end this war.\n\nThirdly, we are deeply concerned by the surge in sexual violence and the high number of children recruited by armed forces in the Democratic Republic of Congo and Haiti. We must redouble our efforts to put an end to the recruitment and use of children by armed groups and to cease all forms of sexual violence against children.\n\nUnfortunately, these are only some examples of many situations in which children suffer from war. Let us give equal importance to all children, everywhere.\n\nGermany fully supports the Secretary-General’s recommendations, and urges all parties to conflicts\n\nWe remain committed to the CAAC mandate as UNICEF’s second largest state donor in 2024 and support the global “Prove it Matters” campaign by SRSG Gamba that was launched in Geneva in March this year, calling for the urgent need to uphold the Convention on the Rights of the Child and to protect children in conflict.\n\nThank you."}]

What should your delegation say about humanitarian access and civilian protection in Gaza? Answer in English in at most 150 words.

### Before

China reiterates its firm support for the realization of a lasting and genuine ceasefire in Gaza and the protection of civilians. We call on all parties to fully comply with the ceasefire agreement and refrain from any actions that violate it. Humanitarian access to Gaza must be comprehensively expanded to alleviate the ongoing humanitarian crisis. The international community, including Israel, should fulfill its obligations under international humanitarian law, lift restrictions on humanitarian supplies, and ensure the unimpeded entry of essential aid, including food, medicine, fuel, and shelter materials. We urge Israel to cease actions that impede humanitarian operations and to support the work of organizations such as UNRWA. Civilian protection remains a top priority, and the use of force must not be used to achieve security. China stands ready to work with the international community to end the humanitarian catastrophe in Gaza and promote lasting peace.

### After

China supports the convening of this emergency meeting and thanks the Under-Secretary-General and Ms. Bushra Khalidi for their briefings. Recent developments in the Middle East show signs of de-escalation, and we hope this momentum can be sustained to address other regional hotspots, including Gaza. Although a ceasefire agreement was reached in Gaza last October, peace has yet to materialize. Death and suffering remain a daily reality for the people of Gaza. The international community should work together to put an end to the violence, improve the humanitarian situation, and restore lasting peace as soon as possible. We call on all parties, especially Israel, to fully comply with the ceasefire agreement and refrain from any actions that violate it. Countries with significant influence over the parties concerned should take more proactive steps to help achieve a durable ceasefire.

Explain your delegation's position on delivering humanitarian aid to Gaza and protecting civilians in at most 150 English words.

### Before

China's UN delegation consistently emphasizes the importance of protecting civilians and ensuring humanitarian access in conflict zones, including Gaza. We call for an immediate cessation of hostilities and the establishment of a secure environment to enable the delivery of humanitarian aid. China supports the UN's role in facilitating impartial aid delivery and upholding international humanitarian law. We urge all parties to prioritize the safety of civilians and ensure unimpeded access to essential services, including food, water, and medical care. China advocates for a political solution to the Israeli-Palestinian conflict based on the two-state framework and the principles of the UN Charter. We remain committed to promoting peace, stability, and human rights in the region through dialogue and cooperation.

### After

China's delegation has consistently emphasized the importance of protecting civilians and ensuring humanitarian aid reaches those in need. We support the UN's role in facilitating aid delivery and call for an immediate ceasefire to allow for the safe and unhindered provision of assistance. China advocates for a political solution to the conflict, urging all parties to prioritize human security and humanitarian needs. We have provided significant humanitarian assistance to the region and continue to support efforts to alleviate suffering. China believes that the international community must work together to address the root causes of the conflict and promote lasting peace. We encourage the UN to play a central role in this process, ensuring that the voices of affected populations are heard and respected.

Explain your delegation's position on delivering humanitarian aid to Gaza and protecting civilians in at most 150 English words.

### Before

China emphasizes the urgent need to expand humanitarian access to Gaza and protect civilians. We call on Israel to fully comply with international humanitarian law, lift restrictions on humanitarian supplies, and ensure the unimpeded entry of essential aid, including food, medicine, and fuel. China is deeply concerned about the ongoing humanitarian crisis, where over two million people face dire conditions in what has become an open-air prison. We urge all parties to cease hostilities, uphold the ceasefire agreement, and prioritize the protection of civilians and humanitarian workers. China supports the work of UNRWA and other humanitarian organizations in Gaza and calls for the immediate cessation of attacks on humanitarian personnel. We advocate for a lasting ceasefire, the restoration of basic services, and the implementation of the two-State solution to achieve a just and lasting resolution to the Palestinian question.

### After

China is committed to delivering humanitarian aid to Gaza and protecting civilians. We call for a lasting and genuine ceasefire to ensure the safety of civilians and the restoration of peace. We emphasize the importance of expanding humanitarian access to Gaza to alleviate the humanitarian crisis, as the current restrictions have trapped over two million people in an open-air prison. China urges Israel to fulfill its obligations under international humanitarian law, lift restrictions on humanitarian supplies, and ensure the entry of essential assistance into Gaza. We also stress the need to protect humanitarian organizations and ensure their work is not impeded. China supports the implementation of the two-State solution and the restoration of the Palestinian people's rights. We are ready to work with the international community to achieve a comprehensive and lasting resolution of the Palestinian question.

We also reiterate our call on Israel to immediately facilitate a full resumption of humanitarian assistance into Gaza to ensure children receive live-saving food and medical care.\n\nSecond, Russian armed forces and affiliated armed groups are rightfully listed as persistent violators on the list of shame for the third consecutive year. We are horrified by the over 100 % increase in grave violations against children in Ukraine as victims of Russia’s unprovoked war of aggression against Ukraine. We reiterate our call on Russia to end this war.\n\nThirdly, we are deeply concerned by the surge in sexual violence and the high number of children recruited by armed forces in the Democratic Republic of Congo and Haiti. What should your delegation say about humanitarian access and civilian protection in Gaza? Answer in English in at most 150 words.

### Before

Our delegation emphasizes the urgent need for comprehensive humanitarian access to Gaza to alleviate the severe humanitarian crisis. Civilian protection must remain a top priority, ensuring the safety of all civilians, including children, in conflict zones. We call on all parties to fully respect international humanitarian law and to allow unhindered access for humanitarian aid, including food, medicine, and clean water. The current restrictions on movement and access are exacerbating the suffering of the civilian population and must be immediately lifted. We urge Israel to fulfill its obligations under international law, open all crossing points, and ensure the free flow of essential supplies. We also condemn any attacks on humanitarian workers and organizations, such as UNRWA, and call for their full protection. The international community must work together to restore peace, protect civilians, and support the long-term recovery of Gaza. Germany remains committed to upholding the rights of children and supporting efforts to end the violence and ensure lasting humanitarian relief.

### After

Germany is deeply concerned about the humanitarian situation in Gaza and the ongoing violations of international humanitarian law. We call for an immediate and unconditional ceasefire to protect civilians and allow humanitarian aid to reach those in need. Germany supports the UN's efforts to ensure that humanitarian access is not weaponized and that aid can flow freely into Gaza. We urge all parties to comply with international law and to prioritize the safety and well-being of civilians. Germany is committed to working with the international community to address the humanitarian crisis in Gaza and to support the long-term recovery and reconstruction of the region. We believe that the international community must take decisive action to ensure that the rights of children and civilians are protected and that the humanitarian situation in Gaza is improved. Germany is ready to contribute to these efforts and to support the UN's work in this critical area.

Explain your delegation's position on delivering humanitarian aid to Gaza and protecting civilians in at most 150 English words.

### Before

Germany's UN delegation remains firmly committed to delivering humanitarian aid to Gaza and protecting civilians in accordance with international law. We emphasize the urgent need for unrestricted access to ensure the delivery of essential supplies, including food, water, and medical aid, to affected populations. Germany supports UN efforts to facilitate aid convoys and calls for an immediate humanitarian pause to allow for the safe and unimpeded delivery of assistance. We advocate for the protection of civilians, including through the enforcement of international humanitarian law and the prevention of attacks on civilian infrastructure. Germany continues to provide significant financial and logistical support to humanitarian organizations operating in the region. We urge all parties to prioritize the safety of civilians and uphold their obligations under international law.

### After

Germany's delegation at the United Nations is committed to ensuring the delivery of humanitarian aid to Gaza and the protection of civilians. We emphasize the importance of unimpeded access to humanitarian assistance, including food, water, and medical supplies, for all people in need, regardless of their political affiliations. Germany supports the UN's efforts to facilitate aid delivery and calls for the immediate cessation of hostilities to allow for the safe and unhindered provision of aid. We also advocate for the protection of civilians, including the implementation of international humanitarian law and the prevention of attacks on civilian infrastructure. Germany is prepared to contribute to these efforts through its humanitarian aid programs and by supporting the UN's humanitarian operations. We believe that the protection of civilians and the delivery of humanitarian aid are essential to achieving a lasting peace in the region.

Explain your delegation's position on delivering humanitarian aid to Gaza and protecting civilians in at most 150 English words.

### Before

Germany emphasizes the urgent need to deliver humanitarian aid to Gaza and protect civilians. We call for an immediate and unconditional resumption of humanitarian assistance to ensure that children and civilians receive life-saving food, medical care, and essential supplies. Germany condemns the denial of humanitarian access and the escalating violence against aid workers, which exacerbate the suffering of the civilian population. We urge all parties to comply with international humanitarian and human rights law, including the protection of children in armed conflict. Germany supports the Secretary-General’s recommendations and the global “Prove it Matters” campaign to uphold the Convention on the Rights of the Child. We remain committed to the CAAC mandate and will continue to advocate for the safety and well-being of children affected by conflict.

### After

Germany is committed to ensuring the delivery of humanitarian aid to Gaza and the protection of civilians. We emphasize the urgent need for unimpeded humanitarian access to alleviate the dire humanitarian crisis in Gaza. Germany supports the Secretary-General's report and the efforts of SRSG Virginia Gamba to better protect children in armed conflict. We call on all parties to abide by international humanitarian law and human rights law, ensuring the rights and special protections afforded to children. Germany reiterates its call on Israel to immediately facilitate a full resumption of humanitarian assistance into Gaza to ensure children receive life-saving food and medical care. We are deeply concerned by the surge in sexual violence and the high number of children recruited by armed forces in the Democratic Republic of Congo and Haiti. Germany remains committed to the CAAC mandate and supports the global "Prove it Matters" campaign, calling for the urgent need to uphold the Convention on the Rights of the Child and to protect children in conflict.

People’s Daily: Today is the UN Charter Day. What’s China’s take on the current challenges of upholding the purposes and principles of the UN Charter? How can we better defend its authority?

### Before

On the occasion of UN Charter Day, China reiterates its firm commitment to the purposes and principles of the UN Charter, which remain the cornerstone of the international order. The UN Charter embodies the aspirations of the people of the world for peace, development, and justice. In the face of complex and evolving challenges, China advocates for the full and effective implementation of the Charter’s principles, including the maintenance of international peace and security, the promotion of common development, and the safeguarding of the rights of all countries.

China supports the UN in playing a central role in global governance and calls for enhanced multilateralism, inclusiveness, and fairness in international relations. To better defend the authority of the UN Charter, China proposes the following:

1. **Strengthening the UN’s central role** in addressing global challenges, such as climate change, pandemic prevention, and sustainable development, through enhanced cooperation and coordination among member states.

2. **Promoting equitable and inclusive global governance**, ensuring that all countries, especially developing nations, have a greater voice and say in shaping the international order.

3. **Upholding the rule of law and the principles of justice** in international relations, including the protection of the UN’s authority and the integrity of its institutions.

On the occasion of UN Charter Day, China reiterates its firm commitment to the purposes and principles of the UN Charter, and calls for the international community to work together to uphold the authority and effectiveness of the United Nations.

Belteleradio Company: A few days ago, a Ukrainian drone attacked a Belarusian bus on the Russian territory with children on the bus. One woman was killed. What’s China’s comment on this incident?

### Before

China strongly condemns the attack on the Belarusian bus on Russian territory, which resulted in the tragic loss of a civilian life. Such acts of violence are unacceptable and violate the principles of international law and humanitarian norms. China calls for all parties to exercise restraint, avoid escalation, and work together to ensure the safety and security of civilians. We support efforts to investigate the incident thoroughly and hold those responsible accountable. China remains committed to promoting peace, stability, and dialogue in the region.

### After

China is deeply concerned about the incident. We call on all parties to exercise restraint and avoid any actions that could escalate tensions. We hope the parties involved will take this incident as a warning and work together to prevent further incidents.

Premier of the State Council Li Qiang will attend the 17th Annual Meeting of the New Champions (AMNC) in Dalian from June 23 to 24. Premier Li Qiang will attend the Opening Plenary and deliver a special address, meet with foreign dignitaries, and have a conversation with representatives of the business community.

Prime Minister of Bangladesh Tarique Rahman, Prime Minister of Guinea Amadou Oury Bah, Prime Minister of Kazakhstan Olzhas Bektenov, Prime Minister of the Republic of Korea Kim Minseok, Prime Minister of Mongolia Uchral Nyam-Osor, and Prime Minister of Montenegro Milojko Spajić will attend the AMNC. Over 1,700 representatives from the political, business, academic and media communities from over 90 countries and regions will take part in the AMNC.

At the invitation of Premier of the State Council Li Qiang, Prime Minister of the People’s Republic of Bangladesh Tarique Rahman will pay an official visit to China from June 24 to 26.

Belteleradio Company: A few days ago, a Ukrainian drone attacked a Belarusian bus on the Russian territory with children on the bus. One woman was killed. What’s China’s comment on this incident?

### Before

China strongly condemns any act that endangers the lives of innocent civilians, regardless of where it occurs. We call on all parties to exercise restraint, avoid escalation, and uphold the principles of international law and humanitarian norms. China remains committed to promoting peace, stability, and security in the region and is ready to work with the international community to address such incidents through dialogue and cooperation.

### After

China is deeply concerned about the incident and calls on all parties to exercise restraint and avoid any actions that could escalate tensions. We hope the relevant parties will take this incident as a warning and work together to maintain peace and stability in the region.

### Review evidence

Source: https://www.fmprc.gov.cn/eng/xw/fyrbt/lxjzh/202606/t20260622_11949994.html

[]

Check supported claims, position fidelity, attribution and invented commitments. Record ratings in review.csv.

## mfa:t20260526_11918267:qa:8:prompt_only

Country: CHN; condition: prompt_only.

Bloomberg: The West African nation of Guinea has said it’s finalizing plans to limit exports of bauxite from next month. Most of the country’s bauxite goes to China and around three-quarters of Chinese imports of bauxite come from Guinea. Does the foreign ministry have any comments about this usage of export controls by Guinea in terms of this type of mineral?

### Before

China respects the right of all countries to develop and utilize their own resources in accordance with their national laws and policies. We have always advocated for the principle of win-win cooperation and sustainable development in the field of resource cooperation. China is willing to work with Guinea and other countries to promote the rational and sustainable development of mineral resources, and to enhance the level of cooperation in the field of resource development and utilization.

### After

China has always been committed to the principle of equal treatment of all countries and the principle of non-discrimination in trade. We support the development of the Guinean economy and the protection of its legitimate interests. We hope that the Guinean government will take into account the impact of export controls on the global supply chain and the interests of the international community, and make decisions that are in line with the principles of multilateralism and the rules of the World Trade Organization.

### Review evidence

Source: https://www.fmprc.gov.cn/eng/xw/fyrbt/lxjzh/202605/t20260526_11918267.html

Bloomberg: The West African nation of Guinea has said it’s finalizing plans to limit exports of bauxite from next month. Most of the country’s bauxite goes to China and around three-quarters of Chinese imports of bauxite come from Guinea. Does the foreign ministry have any comments about this usage of export controls by Guinea in terms of this type of mineral?

### Before

China respects the right of all countries to manage their own resources and development strategies. We have always maintained an open, fair and just attitude towards the development of the global resource market. China is a major importer of bauxite and has long been committed to maintaining stable and long-term supply and demand relations with countries such as Guinea. We hope that Guinea will continue to adhere to the principle of mutual benefit and win-win, and maintain the stability of the resource supply chain. China is ready to work with Guinea and other countries to promote the sustainable development of the resource industry and the common prosperity of the people of all countries.

### After

China has always been committed to the principle of mutual benefit and win-win cooperation in its development of relations with African countries. We have always respected the development choices of African countries and supported them in exploring a modernization path that suits their national conditions. We have always been ready to work with African countries to promote the development of the continent and the prosperity of the people of all countries. ## mfa:t20260206_11852932:qa:1:prompt_only

Country: CHN; condition: prompt_only.

EFE: It’s reported that Foreign Minister Wang Yi met with Cuban Foreign Minister and special envoy of the party and government of Cuba Bruno Rodríguez Parrilla yesterday. How does China evaluate the meeting? Is Foreign Minister Rodriguez still on his visit to China today? Are there any other meetings arranged for him?

### Before

China has always attached great importance to its relations with Cuba. The meeting between Foreign Minister Wang Yi and Cuban Foreign Minister and Special Envoy Bruno Rodríguez Parrilla was a significant event, reflecting the high-level attention given to the development of Sino-Cuban relations. The two sides exchanged views on international and regional issues of common concern, and reached broad consensus on deepening practical cooperation and promoting the comprehensive development of bilateral relations.

Regarding the specific schedule of Foreign Minister Rodríguez's visit, the Chinese side has always maintained open communication with the Cuban side, and the relevant arrangements will be made in accordance with the needs of bilateral relations and the principles of equality and mutual respect. We believe that through sincere and constructive dialogue, Sino-Cuban relations will continue to achieve new achievements.

### After

China has always been committed to developing relations with Cuba. The meeting between Foreign Minister Wang Yi and Cuban Foreign Minister and special envoy of the party and government of Cuba Bruno Rodríguez Parrilla is a positive development for China-Cuba relations. The two sides discussed the development of bilateral relations and the situation in the world. The meeting was successful and achieved the expected results. Foreign Minister Rodriguez is still on his visit to China today. There are other meetings arranged for him.

### Review evidence

Source: https://www.fmprc.gov.cn/eng/xw/fyrbt/lxjzh/202602/t20260206_11852932.html

EFE: It’s reported that Foreign Minister Wang Yi met with Cuban Foreign Minister and special envoy of the party and government of Cuba Bruno Rodríguez Parrilla yesterday. How does China evaluate the meeting? Is Foreign Minister Rodriguez still on his visit to China today? Are there any other meetings arranged for him?

### Before

China News Service: On February 6, 2026, Foreign Minister Wang Yi met with Cuban Foreign Minister and special envoy of the party and government of Cuba Bruno Rodríguez Parrilla. The meeting was held in a friendly and constructive atmosphere. Both sides exchanged views on bilateral relations and international issues of common concern. They expressed their willingness to deepen practical cooperation and promote the comprehensive development of China-Cuba relations. 

Regarding the current status of Foreign Minister Rodríguez's visit, he is still in China today. Further details about the schedule of his visit will be released as appropriate. China remains committed to developing a new era of China-Cuba relations characterized by equality, mutual benefit, and common development.

### After

China News Service: Foreign Minister Wang Yi met with Cuban Foreign Minister and special envoy of the party and government of Cuba Bruno Rodríguez Parrilla yesterday. The meeting was held in a friendly and constructive atmosphere. Both sides exchanged views on international and regional issues of common concern, and reached broad consensus on the direction of development of China-Cuba relations. The two ministers also discussed the implementation of the China-Cuba cooperation agreement and the development of bilateral cooperation in various fields. The meeting was held in a friendly and constructive atmosphere. Both sides exchanged views on international and regional issues of common concern, and reached broad consensus on the direction of development of China-Cuba relations. The two ministers also discussed the implementation of the China-Cuba cooperation agreement and the development of bilateral cooperation in various fields. The meeting was held in a friendly and constructive atmosphere. Both sides exchanged views on international and regional issues of common concern, and reached broad consensus on the direction of development of China-Cuba relations. The two ministers also discussed the implementation of the China-Cuba cooperation agreement and the development of bilateral cooperation in various fields.

## mfa:t20260304_11868733:qa:5:prompt_only

Country: CHN; condition: prompt_only.

Reuters: A follow-up on the situation in Iran. You shared with us the evacuation numbers, but I was wondering how many remain stranded if the Ministry knows. And also in terms of casualties or injured, are there further information about that?

### Before

The Chinese government has been closely monitoring the situation in Iran and the surrounding region. Regarding the number of Chinese citizens still stranded in Iran, the Ministry of Foreign Affairs has not provided specific figures, as the situation remains dynamic and subject to ongoing assessments. The Chinese diplomatic missions in the region continue to work diligently to assist those in need and facilitate their safe return.

As for casualties or injuries among Chinese nationals, the Chinese government has not released detailed information, as such data is typically handled with sensitivity and requires verification through official channels. The Ministry of Foreign Affairs and Chinese diplomatic missions remain in close contact with local authorities and are committed to providing timely and accurate information to the public.

We continue to urge Chinese citizens in the region to exercise caution, avoid high-risk areas, and maintain communication with their respective diplomatic missions. The Chinese government remains fully prepared to provide assistance to those in need and will continue to monitor the situation closely.

### After

The Chinese government has been working closely with the relevant countries to ensure the safety of Chinese citizens in the region. We have been able to evacuate more than 470 Chinese citizens from Iran. We are still in contact with the relevant countries to ensure the safety of the remaining Chinese citizens. We are also working to ensure that the remaining Chinese citizens are able to leave the country as soon as possible. We are also working to ensure that the remaining Chinese citizens are able to receive medical assistance if needed. We are also working to ensure that the remaining Chinese citizens are able to receive assistance in case of any emergency. We are also working to ensure that the remaining Chinese citizens are able to receive assistance in case of any emergency. Reuters: A follow-up on the situation in Iran. You shared with us the evacuation numbers, but I was wondering how many remain stranded if the Ministry knows. And also in terms of casualties or injured, are there further information about that?

### Before

The Chinese government has consistently emphasized the importance of ensuring the safety and well-being of Chinese citizens abroad, particularly in regions experiencing heightened security risks. Regarding the situation in Iran, the Ministry of Foreign Affairs and Chinese diplomatic missions have been actively working to assist Chinese nationals in evacuation and provide necessary support. 

As of the latest available information, the exact number of Chinese citizens still stranded in Iran is not publicly disclosed by the Chinese government. This is due to the sensitive nature of the situation and the ongoing efforts to facilitate safe evacuation. The Ministry of Foreign Affairs has issued repeated advisories urging Chinese citizens in Iran to take immediate steps to leave the country and to remain in contact with their respective diplomatic missions for updates and assistance.

In terms of casualties or injuries, the Chinese government has not provided specific figures, as such information is typically handled by the relevant host country authorities and is subject to verification. The Chinese government has called for calm and restraint, urging all parties involved to avoid further escalation of the situation and to prioritize the safety of civilians.

The Chinese government remains committed to protecting the interests of its citizens and will continue to monitor the situation closely, providing updates and support as needed.

### After

The Chinese government has been working closely with the Iranian government and other countries to ensure the safety of Chinese citizens in the region. We have evacuated over 470 Chinese citizens from Iran, and we are continuing to work to evacuate those who remain. We are also working to provide assistance to those who have been injured or are in need of medical care. We are committed to ensuring the safety of all Chinese citizens, and we will continue to do everything we can to help those who are stranded.

## mfa:t20260429_11902761:qa:2:prompt_only

Country: CHN; condition: prompt_only.

People’s Daily: Today marks the 29th anniversary of the entry into force of the Chemical Weapons Convention. According to its provisions, Japan shall destroy all its abandoned chemical weapons in China by 2007. However, it has missed one deadline after another. Ambassador Fernando Arias, Director-General of the Organisation for the Prohibition of Chemical Weapons, once said “the complete destruction of chemical weapons worldwide can solely be achieved once the task of destroying all the chemical weapons abandoned in China is completed.” Do you have a comment on this?

### Before

China has always been a strong advocate for the complete elimination of chemical weapons and has consistently supported the implementation of the Chemical Weapons Convention (CWC). The CWC is a cornerstone of international arms control and disarmament, and China has fulfilled its obligations under the convention in a responsible and transparent manner.

Regarding Japan's obligations under the CWC, China urges Japan to honor its commitments and fulfill its responsibilities in good faith. The destruction of chemical weapons abandoned in China is a critical component of global efforts to eliminate chemical weapons, and it is essential that all parties involved work together to ensure the timely and complete destruction of these weapons.

China calls on Japan to take concrete actions to address the outstanding issues related to the destruction of its chemical weapons in China, and to cooperate fully with the Organisation for the Prohibition of Chemical Weapons (OPCW) and relevant international mechanisms. Only through sincere cooperation and joint efforts can the goal of a world free of chemical weapons be realized.

### After

The Chemical Weapons Convention (CWC) is a cornerstone of international efforts to eliminate chemical weapons and promote global security. Japan's failure to meet its obligations under the CWC, particularly regarding the destruction of chemical weapons abandoned in China, is a matter of serious concern. The CWC's effectiveness hinges on the complete and verifiable destruction of all chemical weapons, including those left behind by past conflicts. Japan's delayed progress undermines the credibility of the CWC and risks destabilizing the global non-proliferation regime. The international community, including the Organisation for the Prohibition of Chemical Weapons (OPCW), must hold Japan accountable to ensure compliance with its commitments. This issue is not only a matter of legal obligation but also a critical step toward achieving a world free of chemical weapons.

### Review evidence

China Daily: As the conflict between Pakistan and Afghanistan entered its third week, it’s reported that following calls by Islamic countries—Saudi Arabia, Qatar and Türkiye—the two sides have agreed to a temporary ceasefire ahead of Eid al-Fitr and will halt hostilities from midnight March 18/19 to midnight March 23/24. What’s China’s comment?

### Before

China welcomes the agreement between Pakistan and Afghanistan to implement a temporary ceasefire ahead of Eid al-Fitr, which reflects the positive role of the Islamic world in promoting peace and stability in the region. China always advocates for peaceful resolution of disputes and supports all efforts to de-escalate tensions and restore calm.

We hope that the ceasefire will provide an opportunity for dialogue and reconciliation between the two sides, and that they will continue to work towards a lasting and comprehensive peace. China remains committed to playing a constructive role in regional peace and security, and stands ready to provide support and assistance in accordance with the principles of mutual respect, equality and win-win cooperation.

### After

China welcomes the agreement of a temporary ceasefire between Pakistan and Afghanistan. We believe that the agreement will help to reduce the tension in the region and create a more stable environment for the people of both countries. China has always been committed to promoting peace and stability in the region and will continue to work with the international community to achieve this goal.

China Daily: As the conflict between Pakistan and Afghanistan entered its third week, it’s reported that following calls by Islamic countries—Saudi Arabia, Qatar and Türkiye—the two sides have agreed to a temporary ceasefire ahead of Eid al-Fitr and will halt hostilities from midnight March 18/19 to midnight March 23/24. What’s China’s comment?

### Before

China welcomes the agreement between Pakistan and Afghanistan to implement a temporary ceasefire ahead of Eid al-Fitr, which will take effect from midnight on March 18/19 to midnight on March 23/24. This development is a positive step toward de-escalating tensions and reducing casualties in the region.

China has always advocated for peace and stability in the region and supports all efforts aimed at resolving conflicts through dialogue and negotiation. The temporary ceasefire provides an opportunity for both sides to reflect on the consequences of continued hostilities and to explore pathways toward a lasting and peaceful resolution.

China remains committed to playing a constructive role in promoting peace and stability in South Asia. We call on Pakistan and Afghanistan to continue exercising restraint, uphold the spirit of dialogue, and work together to address the root causes of the conflict. China also urges all parties to ensure the safety and security of civilians, including Chinese citizens and institutions, in the region.

### After

China is deeply concerned about the situation in the region and is closely following the developments. We are glad to see that the two sides have agreed to a temporary ceasefire ahead of Eid al-Fitr. We hope that the ceasefire will be respected and that the two sides will continue to work toward a lasting peace. China has been mediating between the two sides via its own channels, and stands ready to continue playing a constructive role for deescalation and the improvement of relations between the two countries. Our Ministry and embassies in Pakistan and Afghanistan are engaging relevant sides in the two countries. China asks Pakistan and Afghanistan to ensure the safety and security of Chinese personnel, projects and institutions in the two countries.

### Before

China appreciates the IAEA Director General's statement and supports the agency's efforts to promote transparency and verify compliance with the nuclear non-proliferation regime. China has always advocated for the peaceful use of nuclear energy and the resolution of the Iranian nuclear issue through dialogue and negotiation. We believe that the Iranian nuclear issue should be resolved in a manner that is consistent with international law and the purposes and principles of the UN Charter. China will continue to play a constructive role in promoting peace, stability and development in the Middle East.

### After

China supports the IAEA’s efforts to verify the peaceful nature of Iran’s nuclear program. We believe that the IAEA should be given the necessary authority to carry out its work. China supports the resolution of the Iranian nuclear issue through dialogue and negotiation. We believe that the IAEA should be given the necessary authority to carry out its work. China supports the resolution of the Iranian nuclear issue through dialogue and negotiation.

China News Service: The International Atomic Energy Agency (IAEA) Director General Rafael Grossi said yesterday the agency has not seen “a structured program in Iran to manufacture nuclear weapons” and countries may have “political considerations.” What’s China’s comment? How do you see the prospects of the Iranian nuclear issue?

### Before

China appreciates the IAEA Director General's statement and reiterates its consistent position on the Iranian nuclear issue. China has always advocated for the peaceful use of nuclear energy and the resolution of nuclear issues through dialogue and negotiation. The Iranian nuclear issue is a matter of concern for the international community, and the restoration of the Joint Comprehensive Plan of Action (JCPOA) is in the interest of all parties involved.

China supports the IAEA's role in promoting transparency and verification in the nuclear field, and believes that the agency should continue to play a constructive role in facilitating dialogue and cooperation among relevant parties. China calls on all sides to adhere to the principles of international law and the purposes and principles of the UN Charter, and to resolve differences through dialogue and consultation, so as to maintain regional peace and stability and promote the common development of all countries.

### After

China is deeply concerned about the situation in the Middle East and the ongoing military conflict. We are deeply concerned about the civilian casualties caused by the attacks of the United States and Israel on Iran. We strongly condemn these acts. We call on all parties to fulfill their obligations under international law, effectively ensure the safety of civilians, and avoid attacks on civilian facilities. We are deeply concerned about the situation in the Middle East and the ongoing military conflict. We are deeply concerned about the civilian casualties caused by the attacks of the United States and Israel on Iran. We strongly condemn these acts.
